                    During the course of the U. S. A ttorney s con erenc

                                                                                  Hollo.... _____ _
                                                                                                        e
      on 10/7/55, I discussed the above-captioned subject with U. S.              G•""r '-'---
                                                                                                                                              ·,;
     Attorney Peter-Mills of Portland along with Mr. J.:>sepllMcGuire, General 1
      Council of the Pure Food and Drug Administration. Reich is the subject of
      an injunction proceeding banning his shipment of a s:>-called :>rg:>ne energy
      accumlator. Tllis was the subject of a press release by the Attorn"y General
     on Z/10 /54. Reich is currently being brought up on a c:mtempt pr:>ceeding
     because he has not followed the terms of an injuncti:>n and in this connection,
                                                                                                                                              •
     the Bureau has received various letters from Reich which we have n·ot
     acknowledged but which we have referred directly to the Pure Food and Drug                                                               ''


                                                                                                                                         --
     Administration f::>r such action as they care4Jo take.
                                              ,                                                                                          ~·


                    J:>seph McGuire states he has the Phot:>stats sent by the Bure<J.u
     land believes that the communications contain some admissions against interest
     ion the part of Reich and his associate. McGuire plans to make reference to
                                                                                                                                         -
     !Reich's correspondence to the Bureau during the contempt proceeding provided
      the Bureau has no objections to his doing so. McGuire further stated if the case
      ever does go to trial he will possibly want to utilize the originals of Reich's                                                         •
                                                                                                                                              ~
      correspondence to the Bureau although he has no need for the originals at this                                                          •
      time. He asked to be advised in the next several days telephonically {Code 176,       •                                                 '
      Extensi::m 3Z37) as to whether the Bureau has any objections to his m<J.king reference

  t   to correspondence which Reich has had with. the Bureau and which we• referred
      without acknowledgment directly to the Pure Food and Drug Administration.
                                                                                           ·~                                                 ''
                                                                                                                                               ··
                                                                                                                                                   I
                                                                                                                                                   ·'
                    It is recommended that the Investigative Division review the file
                                                                                                                                                   :<
     -on this matter SD that Joseph McGuire can be informed as to whether or not any                                                           ~)
      reasons exist why he should not make reference to this correspondence in the                                                             f
      contempt proceeding which will be heard one week from this Friday, namely,
      ll/4/55.
